#205297 265 2006-07-20T23:45:03Z dejablue
1. I dont know if this would be possible... but howbout being able to tell when someone is using fbf or not by the demo data... there have been tons of arguments on whether or not fbf was used in 'dedication oppurtunity' maps so i thought there should be a way to settle the argument

2. Different colors of gold pieces (ok so its a oxymoron... you know what i mean)... on one of my latest maps, Triple Threat , there were different paths that the ninja could take and three different exits to the level... i tried to differ each path by putting no gold for one path, singular pieces of gold for another path, and double pieces of gold for another... but the level got so confusing... just go look at the map if you dont understand what im trying to explain here...
2a. And plus, different colors of gold might make the game look a bit more 'attractive' without adding too much complication like a background scene or something

3. Changing Ned around a bit... i come upon many problems when trying to make a level... howbout 1/2 and 1/4 snap on tiles... and also on regular, trap, and open doors (basically all doors)... as well as slanted doors and one-ways... and then maybe different shapes of tiles... only a couple more though, i dont want to see a full screen tile select page... thatd be confusing
3a. oh and almost forgot... definitly object copying in Ned... thatd help tons

ok well those are my three major suggestions... thanx for reading them if you've read this far

#10199 1 2006-07-21T20:50:41Z dejablue
I've been thinking about this competition idea for a while now...

Rules-
1. Must be a dda (can be hold left, right, or nothing)
1. Map must be in a 10x10 or smaller area (if the ninja goes anywhere outside that 10x10 area the map does not count)
2. You can use as many objects and tiles within the 10x10 area as you want
3. You can edit anything in the level data manually
4. Can include glitches (this isnt one of those things where if it's not used in metanet it cant be used here)
5. Cannot be an infinite dda
5. Um yea thats about it

Ideal map would have-
1. As many close calls as possible
2. Different types of propulsion
3. More than just one rocket (and also not just two or more rockets in the same spot)
4. Gold delay wont effect your score much... but it will be used as a tie-breaker if needed (less gold is better)
5. Oh and cheating death is always good... might add a bonus point or two

Scoring-
Variety of Propulsion: X/5
Number of rockets: X/5
Length: X/10
Creativity: X/10
Quality and Quantity of close calls: X/20
NOTE: Total score will be X/50

Judges-
Dejablue4 (me)
Ask if you want to be a judge

Reward-
You suggest, I'll listen and agree or disagree

Here's a sample map that would fit the rules that i made- http://numa.notdot.net/map/52526

Deadline- Friday, August 4

You can submit up to 5 levels
Current submissions-

#239421 16 2006-10-11T00:31:26Z dejablue
Quote: Twistkill, October 10, 2006 07:19 pm
Quote: dejablue4, October 10, 2006 08:07 pm
no, it is... maybe im not putting it in the best words... could someone back me up on this and explain it better than i can?
Okay, when you get to the last episode in a column, (Episodes 0-9 is a column, 20-29 is a column, etc.) you only have to reach the last level of that episode to unlock the flavor for beating that column. For example, if you have reached 29-4, you don't have to complete it for you to unlock the flavor for that column. You can close N, reopen it, and go to the configure menu. Your unlocked flavor will be there. A weird bug, but it's completely true.

Here is a PM that I sent to both Sweep and Maestro:

Quote
Ok, look. I have a huge confession to make and I understand if it is unforgivable, but all I ask of you is to hear me out. When I joined numa at the start of the summer, I thought it was the greatest website ever. I started out making poor maps of course, but I took any constructive criticism i got to heart and gradually my maps started to get better. But I still wasn't satisfied with the ratings (yes, I was obssessive over ratings) that I was receiving. So then I made a ridiculous decision and created two other accounts, qwerty12 and connecticutt12. These two accounts were created solely to rate up my own maps. Before these two accounts came into existence, I had also created a "joke" collaboration account with my friend, swanky23, called tk^-3 (I was ridiculously immature when I made that account  ). I began rating every single map I made 5/5 with all three accounts. I was smart about it, I don't believe anybody suspected me of multi-accounting (except for xdude_, I give kudos to that guy, he confronted me personally by PM as a friend and I lied to him. Geez I was so stupid.), but as time passed and my experience on numa became greater and greater, that guilty conscience of mine started messing with me. I knew it was wrong to rate up my own maps, but I was obssessed with high ratings.

I have since then stopped rating my own maps up. I now know what a joke ratings are. I know you might be thinking "Oh, it's all talk, he's still obssessed with ratings." And for all I know, you might be right. I could be bullsh*tting myself and still be hooked on ratings, but all I ask for is another chance. If I continue to rate my maps up, you can just check the IP addresses (I think you can do that anyways) and ban me for good.

Also, if it's not too much trouble, if you do let me stay, could you reset all of my ratings to 0? Or, you can just delete my dejablue4, qwerty12, connecticutt12, and tk^-3 accounts... and allow me to register for a completely new one. You can do anything you want, except one thing. Please do not IP ban me. Thanks.

Please reply and let me know what you're going to do with my accounts. I've confessed all of myself over to you. Do with me what you want. Thanks.

I just hope that everyone will think of me and my maps the same way they were thought of before today. Please, I was young (numa-wise) when I created those accounts, I didn't know any better. Then copy and paste Project 1 (or whatever you want the name to be) into the "Map Name" box. Then select a category from the drop down menu in the "Category" box. Then enter a description in the "Description" box. Don't mess with the "N Version" drop down menu. Keep that at 1.4.

#273272 116 2006-12-19T05:55:02Z dejablue
For the past couple days, as suggestion after suggestion kept popping up across the forum, I have been thinking of ways to stop multi-accounting. I thought, well of course I should be able to come up with a way, since I was the one who actually did the crime. I had first-hand experience with what the system really was like and how easily abused it was. But after brainstorming for some time, I have come up with nothing. Nothing new anyway. But I've decided to just flashback on those days when I abused the system, maybe someone will see something I don't.

So I got my dejablue4 account. I submitted 8 maps all within a timespan of 10 minutes. I realized only two of them were put onto the newest page. I was all like WTF?! But then my friend told me the rules and everything, and it went on like that for a couple months. Then I wanted higher ratings and all, so I created qwerty12. I registered for it with the same email I used to register for dejablue4. If I remember correctly, it came up with a warning saying that the email I had used was already in use by an existing member. I easily got around this by creating a new email account (not hard to do, at all) and using it for the "Confirmation Email". I did the same for connecticutt12 a couple days later. And from there, nothing would have stopped me if my guilty conscience hadn't told me to stop.

Meh, why do I feel like that story up there was useless . I was thinking maybe it would spark something in someone's mind. Maybe about confirmation emails? Or something along those lines... I don't know. Well, at least I tried.

#273907 3 2006-12-20T18:11:41Z dejablue
For each episode, time carries over. So if you completed 40-0 and 40-1, the time you had at the end of 40-1 will be your starting time for 40-2. With 0.374 seconds, you'll have to start the entire episode over again. It just isn't possible to complete this level with that little time to start.

Edit: jtbandes beat me to it. Just listen to what he said.

#13742 1 2007-01-28T05:21:33Z dejablue
Ok, so I am MidnightGetaway.

It all started when I confessed to multi-accounting. That seems ages ago, to me at least it does. The respect I had gotten on my dejablue4 account by rating up my own maps was anything but genuine. I kept thinking that if I hadn't rated my maps up, I would have never gotten exposed to the people here. So I decided, to make a fresh start. To gain respect from the Numa community the right way. Without cheating any rating system, but just by being a good member and making good maps and comments.

So, after talking it over with Sweep and Maestro, MidnightGetaway was created. I asked that dejablue4 be kept disabled, and it still is. As far as I know, nobody will ever log in as dejablue4 ever again. So there's my story. Don't hurt yourself.

Oh, and one more thing, props to IAABH for identifying me and putting it down on a comment. I'm positive that many of you had already known (or been suspicious) that MidnightGetaway was not new. And I'm positive a portion of that many had a suspicion that it was me.

Well, that's all for now. Just thought you guys had the right to know. I have kept many secrets from you guys, and some I am slowly letting go of, but others, I don't see myself telling people over the internet anytime soon. At all.

#14505 1 2007-03-19T17:31:12Z dejablue
http://metanet.2.forumer.com/index.php?showtopic=19360

The Mirage Explosion: The Last Lap

It is complete. Team Mirage's first ever collaboration map-pack is finally finished. After nearly six months of continuous mapping, the end product is here. The mapping is finished. The playtesting is coming to a close. The finishing touches are being finished. The release date has been set. The only thing left is for us to wait. It's been 164 days since we first announced the beginning of this pack. 12 more days will feel like nothing.

The Mappers
~Brttrx
~Infinitum (since added)
~DemonzLunchBreak (since retired)
~Cooby
~Shortshift
~MidnightGetaway

The Final Rundown
~Eight columns of madness.
~Three hundred and ninety four playable maps consisting of actions, puzzles, races, and anything in between.
~Six non-playable maps.
~All four-hundred maps are collaborations.

The Prizes, Awards, and Accolades
~First person to send in completion demos for all three hundred and ninety four playable maps.
~First person to send in all-gold completion demos for all three hundred and ninety four playable maps.
~Second person to send in completion demos for all three hundred and ninety four playable maps.
~Prizes will be given to the three people mentioned above as soon as their demos have been confirmed.
~Prizes will include dedication maps, signature tags, and a secret prize.

The Deadline
~September 11, 2007.
~It has finally been set in stone.
~Yes, nine-eleven.
~Think of the world.

The Mirage Explosion: The Preview


What is it-
~A level pack.
~Consisting of anything and everything from puzzles to races.
~Every map will be a collaboration map.
~This is no Mirage, it's the real deal.

Who is involved-
~DemonzLunchBreak.
~Brttrx.
~Cooby.
~Shortshift.
~MidnightGetaway.
~Infinitum.
~Any playtesters we decide upon.
~A couple of side-people

What to expect-
~8 full columns.
~That's right, 400 levels.
~Action, Puzzle, Race, and anything in between.
~DemonzLunchBreak's all-out amazingness.
~Brttrx's simplicity.
~Cooby's complexity.
~Shortshift's rocking tilesets (pun intended).
~MidnightGetaway's object obssession.
~An unmatched and absolutely original style in the entirety of the level pack.

Deadline?
~Current estimation: August 30, 2007.
~The deadline estimation will change constantly. Nearly every couple days. Be sure to check back often.
~Just remember that these are 400 full-blown collaboration maps.
~It will come soon enough. Be patient. Thank you.

News-
~March 19, 2007: We begin our journey.
~April 30, 2007: Disaster strikes as DemonzLunchBreak announces he can no longer continue with the pack. He will be missed.
~May 6, 2007: Midnight's laptop crashes and burns. Maps saved. Only a slight delay.
~June 23, 2007: Update on status. Brttrx is back to chugging out maps after takin' off for a month. Demonz is still technically 'retired' but still tosses in a random map every now and then. Cooby has been inactive for a little bit because of other things going on in his life. Shortshift pops in every couple weeks or so to dump some incomplete maps on us. We're having difficulties producing mass quantities of maps at a quick rate, but we're moving along. We'll get there eventually.
~June 24, 2007: With the afore mentioned difficulties, Mirage was in need of another member. After a couple PMs, I received an acceptance of the position. For now, the sixth member will remain unknown.
~June 24, 2007: Seven? Most definitely.
~June 25, 2007: Okay eight's enough.
~June 26, 2007: Apparently, eight's too many. I had a feeling. Well, now there are only two extra mapmakers in addition to the original five. Makes seven in all. Progress should be sped up immensely.
~July 3, 2007: Midnight's leaving for a camp in Michigan. He'll be back July 21. The others will continue to work.
~July 24, 2007: Slowed down a bit while Midnight was at camp. But now the pace has picked up once again.
~July 26, 2007: Woah, Midnight had a mental breakdown today. He accidentally erased 70 of Mirage's maps from his textfiles. It was early, give me a break. I was able to recover and remake about 60 of the maps. Close call.
~July 28, 2007: It is not set in stone yet, but most of Team Mirage has agreed to cut down the number of maps in The Mirage Explosion to 400. That means that The Mirage Remnant (100 maps) will be released about two months after n1.5 comes out. Deadline for the Explosion updated drastically.
~July 31, 2007: It is now set in stone. The Mirage Explosion will have 400 maps. Deadline changed to late September.
~August 6, 2007: Brttrx is a God. Infinitum comes back from a week's vacation (I think). Cooby's still busy with work. Shortshift is disappearing slowly. Demonz is, uh, retired. And I'm here. Woo, update on our statuseses.

Until next time, we will see you later. While you wait for the Mirage Explosion, don't hurt yourself. Think of the world.

#15738 1 2007-06-17T05:31:13Z dejablue
This is the first time I've actually really been to the "Serious Discussion" subforum. I'm not a very deep thinker. I live life on the surface and usually don't try to mess with the stuff underneath. But here goes anyways...

Some 'long' time ago, back during the river valley civilizations, humans hadn't discovered water-travel over large bodies of water. Then we created boats and each individual civilization was linked with the other.
Some 'recent' time ago, us humans had never considered traveling through space. We didn't even know there was a space. Then we created space shuttles and other things and we're now beginning to reach out to other planets in our solar system. Sooner or later, my guess is later, we'll become more advanced with space travel and be able to link with other 'civilizations' out there in the universe*.
Some time ago, humans didn't believe we could travel across water. This was proved wrong. Some time ago, humans didn't believe we could travel through the air. This was proved wrong. Some time ago, humans didn't believe we could travel into space. This was proved wrong. Currently, humans do not believe we can travel through time. Will this ever be proved wrong? If so, which dimension will be the limit. Which dimension will prove literally 'impossible' for humans to travel through. Or have we already reached our limit. Is it 'impossible' for humans to travel through time as we wish?


*Here are my thoughts on the question, "Are there other civilizations out there in space other than us?"

Yes. I do believe there are. The universe is proven to be large. Filled with trillions of stars. The planet Earth revolving around one star is mind-bogglingly small. I just cannot wrap my head around the fact that there is nobody else in the rest of the universe. I have heard that there was a one in a billion chance that the earth turned out the way it did. All of conditions that made the Earth what it is today were very improbable. But it was still probable. And considering the vast size of the universe, how improbable would it be that the same or similar conditions did not occur somewhere else. Not very, I say. So yes, I do believe there are other living beings out there in the universe.
Assuming that we do eventually become advanced enough to travel through space at relative ease, human instinct would be to search for these scattered 'civilizations' across the universe. Just like how humans on Earth searched for scattered 'civilizations' across the globe after sea-travel was discovered. Also assuming that we find these civlizations, how would we react. How would they react. Would we find ourselves in intergalactic wars relatively similar to the wars we find on earth today? After discovering other humans on earth, we eventually broke out in wars, treaties, friendships, enemies. After discovering other living beings in the universe, would we eventually break out in wars and treaties?

I'm sorry this is all so disorganized. I just wrote down thoughts as they poured out of my head. And it is getting late, so I'll have to check up on this tomorrow. So long for now. Give me your thoughts, if you would. Thanks.
#332451 4 2007-06-18T04:03:22Z dejablue
Quote: Tsukatu, June 17, 2007 03:32 am
Time is not quite so physical, so it's not really a good idea to think about traveling through time as we travel through water, air, and space. The way we interact with water, air, and space is not the same way we interact with time. Namely, because time isn't physical, we can't play around with it and figure out a way to control it.

True, but that's kind of a point I'm trying to make. Take a step back in time. Imagine how unbelievably mindblowing it would've been to see a friend floating on water, a feat that was proved to be 'impossible' according to the scientific knowledge gathered at the time. Now back to the present. To this day, science has more or less proven time travel to be 'impossible'. But what's to say that we humans are being ignorant and just haven't uncovered and gathered the knowledge needed to accomplish another feat that is currently thought to be 'impossible'.

#17170 1 2007-09-11T03:59:11Z dejablue
The Story.
~I'll keep it short.
~March 19, 2007: Brttrx, DemonzLunchBreak, Cooby, Shortshift, and myself were just fooling around in an AIM chatroom. We submitted about 10 maps to Numa that night in a 30 minute period. This was what originally sparked the idea of The Mirage Explosion. From that moment on, I compiled every map that was made by any of Team Mirage. Our goal was to have 500 when all was said and done.
~April 30, 2007: DemonzLunchBreak retires from mapmaking. Some of his maps that he worked on before announcing his retirement are in the pack.
~June 24, 2007: Infinitum accepts the position as the Sixth Member of Mirage. He helped immensely.
~July 28, 2007: We decrease the initial goal of 500 maps down to 400 maps.
~August 26, 2007: We reach the plateau of 400 maps.
~September 11, 2007: Head for shelter. The Mirage Explosion has begun.

The Contributors.
~Mappers (aka Team Mirage).
~Brttrx.
~DemonzLunchBreak.
~Cooby.
~Shortshift.
~Infinitum.
~MidnightGetaway.
~Playtesters.
~Hendor and Qix.
~George and Mongy.
~Yahoozy_guacamole and Lord_day.
~Others.
~Tileset Signature Maker (maps 78-4 to 79-4): uNcoNditioNal.
~Signature Tag Maker: jtbandes.

The Dedications.
~I asked each member of Team Mirage who they would like to dedicate the entire map-pack to.
~Brttrx replied, "To weed, for helping me get through the pack."
~Demonz replied, "To abstract!"
~Cooby replied, "To brttrx, shortshift, demonz, and midnightgetaway."
~Shortshift replied, "To the victims of Nine-Eleven. May they never be forgotten."
~Infinitum replied, "To my penis."
~And I decided to dedicate the pack to xdude. Wherever he may be now, I wish him the best in life. He was so helpful to me in my earlier days, and I thank him gratefully for that. Keep on rockin' on xdude.

The Pack.
~Okay, so here it is.
~8 columns. 80 episodes. 400 maps. 400 collaboration maps.
~394 playable maps. 6 non-playable maps.

The Competitions. The Prizes. The Accolades.
~First person to send in all-gold* completion demos for all 394 playable maps will receive:
~Four collaboration maps from Team Mirage dedicated to him/her on Numa.
~Signature tag customized by Team Mirage.
~Mystery Prize: Announced when the winner is confirmed.
~*Note: All-gold is possible on 59-3: Iron Curtain, even if it may not seem so at first glance.
~First person to send in completion demos for all 394 playable maps will receive:
~Three collaboration maps from Team Mirage dedicated to him/her on Numa.
~Signature tag customized by Team Mirage.
~Second person to send in completion demos for all 394 playable maps will receive:
~Three collaboration maps from Team Mirage dedicated to him/her on Numa.
~First person to correctly guess (via PM to Dejablue) which map lord_day helped make will receive:
~One collaboration map from Team Mirage dedicated to him/her on Numa.
~P.S. You are allowed one guess per PM. And nobody better spam up my PM box. Wait until I reply, then you can take another guess. Ignoring this rule will result in disqualification.

The Format.
~00-0 to 66-1: Actions, Puzzles, and anything in between (332 maps).
~66-2 to 69-4: Races and Race hybrids (18 maps).
~70-0 to 78-3: The Finale. Saved the best for last (44 maps).
~78-4 to 79-4: The Credits. Courtesy of uNcOnDiTiOnAl (6 maps).

Extra Tid-Bits.
~Some of the puzzles are quite tough to figure out. I will submit most of them to Numa within the next couple of months with their respective spoiler demos. Just in case you get stuck.
~Team Mirage is currently working on a new project. For now, it will remain a mystery as to what this project is. The only thing I'll say is that it is not a map-pack.
~However, Team Mirage is also planning to create a follow-up map-pack to The Mirage Explosion. It will not be released until after n1.5 though.

And without further ado, I present to you The Mirage Explosion. Enjoy it. Don't hurt yourself. And until next time, think of the world.

I think the world will end on January 30, 2008.

If I'm right, I'll die a genius. Everyone else will die with me saying "I told you so" over and over again in their brains.
If I'm wrong on the otherhand (which is a slight possibility); I live, the world doesn't end, and everything turns out for the better.

It's a win-win situation. Sort of.

But back to the whole 2012 thing being the end of the Mayan calendar and whatnot. It reminds me a bit of how people went about the Y2K bug back in 1999. A small population of people created a theory with "false facts" that the world was going to end. Then it chain-reacted throughout a larger part of the world population (as is human nature... you tell the world something bad's gonna happen, humans freak out. It's just how we are). And then 2000 comes about, and absolutely nothing happens. To be honest with you, I'd just let it rest for now. If the sun, moon, and stars lose exactly one-third of their light 9 months into 2009, then we can start worrying. But not until then.

The Buzz Bracket.

Goal and Purpose.
~To crown a map as champion of all playable maps.
~To have fun seeing maps go head to head.
~To be as voter friendly as possible.

What is it.
~A Bracket Tournament.
~Either 32 or 16 maps in the bracket, pending on quantity of nominations.

How's it gonna go down.
~From now until February 16, 2008; my inbox on the forums will be open to nominations.
~In other words, nominate your favorite playable maps of all-time.
~You can nominate as many maps as you want, but keep it somewhat reasonable (as in under 50 nominations per person please).
~Rankings for the bracket will be determined by the number of nominations each map received.
~Ties will be broken by Team Mirage.

What I want you to do.
~Get onto NUMA.
~Make a list of your favorite playable maps currently on NUMA.
~PM me the list (maximum of 50 maps and minimum of 1 please).
~And have some fun doing it.

Team Mirage.
Brttrx, DemonzLunchBreak, Infinitum, Cooby, Shortshift, MidnightGetaway.
